{"forms":[{"form":"legends","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"As a child she had nothing to read but the legends of early Christian martyrs.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["The life story of a saint (such stories are often embellished, but any kind is called a legend)."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"The legend of Troy was discovered to have a historical basis.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["An unrealistic story depicting past events.","A story of unknown origin describing plausible but extraordinary past events."],"synonyms":[{"word":"myth"}],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"the legend of Robin Hood","type":"example"}],"glosses":["An unrealistic story depicting past events.","A plausible story set in the historical past, but whose historicity is uncertain."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"The 1984 Rose Bowl prank has spawned many legends. Symbols: tan, tg."],"topics":["mathematics","sciences"]},{"glosses":["A course suddenly and unexpectedly divergent from one which was previously being followed (especially one embarked on before reaching the end of the previous course)."],"qualifier":"transferred sense"},{"examples":[{"text":"I believe we went off onto a tangent when we started talking about monkeys on unicycles at his retirement party.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A course suddenly and unexpectedly divergent from one which was previously being followed (especially one embarked on before reaching the end of the previous course).","A new topic of discussion unrelated to the one which came before it, arrived at due to a single (peripheral or trivial) point in common with it."],"qualifier":"transferred sense"},{"glosses":["A visual interaction between two or more lines or edges that creates a perceived relationship between them, often in a way that the artist did not intend."],"topics":["art","arts"]},{"glosses":["A small metal blade in a clavichord or other instrument that strikes the strings to produce sound."],"topics":["entertainment","lifestyle","music"]}],"sounds":[{"enpr":"tăn'jənt"},{"ipa":"/ˈtæn.d͡ʒənt/"}],"upstream_line":150090,"upstream_offset":679652899,"upstream_record_sha256":"95a13ef6f537d34a8a821e0146b0f6446f86c6bb05c5f232b3ff12a842e7170c","word":"tangent"}
